- Optimized source-only **Viewer** scrolling without changing page layout or rendering requests. Each document/scale layout now retains page offsets, bottoms, height, and maximum width; binary lookups select the existing half-viewport/one-and-a-half-viewport render range and current physical page. Exact gap, boundary, zoom-anchor, page-position, and request-cancellation behavior remains covered. Reference comparisons span mixed sizes at 1,500 and 65,536 pages; a proxy check performs fewer than 80 indexed reads for one 65,536-page visible/current lookup. That is an algorithmic check, not a frame-rate or memory benchmark. The full frontend suite passed 191 tests across 43 files, and the production build transformed 1,936 modules in 2.79 seconds. A local browser harness with synthetic 1,500-page data scrolled to physical page 750, mounted pages 749–750, and recorded three mock render calls with no browser errors. It does not exercise native rendering or establish large-document viewer-height support.

- Virtualized the source-only **Pages** sidebar. It preserves physical page selection and exact secondary labels while mounting `ceil(clientHeight / 96) + 12` nearby rows plus up to two pinned current/focused rows. Roving Up/Down/Home/End focus can cross virtual boundaries; Enter or Space activates the focused physical page, and Page Up/Page Down remain workspace navigation. External page updates recenter the list without taking focus, and tab/revision/page-count changes reset its local scroll and roving state. Scrolling does not call the viewer or native page-label API. The full frontend suite passed 184 tests across 42 files, and the production build transformed 1,936 modules in 3.44 seconds. The final standalone debug/no-bundle gate built 1,936 frontend modules in 2.77 seconds and native code in 9.94 seconds; its 30,177,792-byte executable SHA-256 is `1D2BB90033B69E8498F6D2AC415DC2022E265685B0E1153AD5C2FC29C6A5117B`, with exact current JavaScript, CSS, and transformed HTML assets verified embedded. A local browser harness with synthetic data mounted 17 rows for 65,536 pages; End focused page 65,536 and Enter selected it with 9 final rows mounted. That harness has no native calls and does not establish end-to-end 65,536-page viewer navigation.

- Hardened source-only **Create a PDF from an image** output preflight. The selected PDF filename, existing target, and output folder are now checked before reading or decoding the source image, so invalid save destinations stop before expensive conversion work. The focused native gate passed 8 tests with 0 failures; the clean full native gate passed 164 tests with 0 failures and 1 ignored test in 401.94 seconds. The standalone debug/no-bundle build exited 0 with 1,934 frontend modules (3.59 seconds frontend, 13.25 seconds native); its 30,173,696-byte executable SHA-256 is `8BC94E12BCD056CF20FC99A8D29BFD4903491BDD3F23CED02ECAFB81AB0052FF`. Exact current JavaScript, CSS, and HTML asset Brotli bytes were verified embedded. The earlier 176-test frontend suite remains prior evidence; no frontend test rerun accompanied this native path-only update. A separate forms audit raised unverified hypotheses, but its small fixture did not reproduce an omission and no validated forms change landed after the review tool blocked follow-up.

- Added source-only **Export page as PNG**. It captures the active document ID/revision and current zero-based physical page, exports exactly that edited page at 72, 150, or 300 DPI, and rechecks the native receipt before showing the output path. Pixels use `ceil(points × dpi / 72)`; native guards a 16,384-pixel edge, 32,000,000 pixels, a 128 MiB copied BGRA bitmap buffer, and a 256 MiB encoded PNG. That bitmap guard is not a bound on all temporary allocations or total RSS. Current crop, rotation, supported annotations, and supported form appearances flatten onto white 8-bit RGB with rounded `pHYs` density metadata; editable text, OCR, vectors, and extra pages are absent. Encrypted, restricted, signed, certified, stale, malformed, page-tree-disagreed, or unsupported PDFs are refused. The source tab, dirty/history state, and recent files remain unchanged. Five focused native tests, the full native gate (163 passed, 0 failed, 1 ignored in 448.72 seconds), and 176 frontend tests across 41 files pass. A mocked browser harness exercised keyboard DPI, raw physical-page payloads, success/cancel/stale receipts, and source-session preservation, not Windows picker or installed-app behavior. Independent output checks confirmed 2×1 alpha pixels and an edited 792×306 page with valid 72-DPI `pHYs` 2,835. The standalone debug/no-bundle build exited 0 with 1,934 frontend modules (2.74 seconds frontend, 20.15 seconds native); its 30,172,672-byte executable SHA-256 is `D1F1AF4439A2B0586D331761AECDE06B605B4278698E0EC117692DC47F1660EA`. Fresh `index-CwP8sZXB.js` Brotli bytes were verified embedded with the `export_page_image` marker. `png` 0.18.1 remains the only new direct root dependency and is locked; notices, inventory, and source archives are unchanged. Windows picker and installed-app verification remain open. See the [PNG export guide](tools/export-page-image.md).

- Added source-only **Create a PDF from an image**. The UI opens one PNG or JPEG source picker and one new-file PDF save flow, with Letter/A4 sizing, Auto/portrait/landscape orientation, a default Letter/Auto/36-point policy, finite 0–72 point margins, contain-and-center placement, and upscaling without cropping. Native conversion applies EXIF orientation, composites transparency onto white, normalizes to 8-bit RGB, refuses animation, multi-picture data, malformed/trailing input, and bounded oversize images, then validates one image XObject and one page before publishing a clean new output session. ICC color profiles and image metadata are not retained, the source file and open tabs remain unchanged, and native desktop picker/rejection interaction remains unverified. Focused UI checks cover exact options, blank margins, cancellation, failure retry, busy/duplicate guards, and clean output activation; the frontend suite passed 169 tests across 39 files and the production build passed with 1,933 modules. The focused native gate passed 9 tests, and the full native gate passed 158 tests (0 failed, 1 ignored) in 726.02 seconds. The standalone Tauri debug/no-bundle build exited 0 with 1,933 frontend modules (5.56 seconds frontend, 35.42 seconds native); the 29,996,032-byte executable SHA-256 is `1C0D41F0E5CB0DA5A2F6FD07EB44985CFBF4B037FD4E401210CFA6B9AF3A55C5`, and its embedded `index-89AGNv3S.js` Brotli bytes include the create command marker. A mocked browser harness verified the visual policy, keyboard validation, and raw command options. This source feature is newer than the unpublished 0.2.6 draft; see the [Create PDF from image guide](tools/create-pdf.md) and [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Added source-only **batch crop**. Organize Pages applies one finite, non-negative top/right/bottom/left point inset set to selected physical pages, using each page’s current displayed bounds and requiring at least 1 point in both retained dimensions. Native validates every target before one undoable commit or no change; mixed sizes or rotations show dimensional ranges only, and crop hides content rather than redacting it. Focused UI checks passed 15 tests, the full frontend suite passed 161 tests across 37 files, the production frontend build passed with 1,932 modules, the focused native batch gate passed 3 tests, and the full native gate passed 149 tests (0 failed, 1 ignored) in 462.16 seconds. The standalone Tauri debug/no-bundle build passed with 1,932 frontend modules (2.52s frontend, 19.67s native). A mocked local-browser harness verified the preview and raw physical-page request; native desktop interaction remains unverified. See the [Organize Pages guide](tools/organize-pages.md).

- Added source-only **page labels**. The native layer reads bounded PDF page-label number trees and projects complete source labels onto the current edited page plan; the UI validates document ID, revision, page count, and contiguous physical indices before displaying labels in Pages, Organize pages, and the status bar. The projection is internal and tested against synthetic plans; labeled-PDF structural edits that reorder or repeat pages remain refused. Empty labels are explicit, duplicate/numeric/Unicode/whitespace text is preserved, physical navigation stays authoritative, and malformed, partial, stale, encrypted, oversized, or unavailable results fall back to physical numbers. The frontend suite passed 159 tests and the production frontend build passed; the focused native page-label gate passed 9 tests and the full native gate passed 146 tests (0 failed, 1 ignored) in 501.21 seconds. A mocked local-browser harness checked rendered labels and physical selection; the standalone Tauri debug/no-bundle build also passed with 1,932 frontend modules (3.31s frontend, 42.24s native). Native desktop interaction remains unverified. This source feature is newer than the unpublished 0.2.6 draft; see the [Page labels guide](tools/page-labels.md).

- Extended source-only **Fill forms** to a strict single-select choice-field subset. Supported dropdowns and fully visible list boxes show exact source display labels, keep opaque option IDs in patches, and map them to the source PDF's private export values in native code. A blank source choice can be changed to a selection but not cleared. The parser validates the complete tagged request before writing, and regenerates only a semantically changed choice's normal appearance; unchanged submitted text, checkbox, radio, and choice fields keep their original appearance and state objects. The frontend suite passed 153 tests and the production frontend build passed; the focused native choice gate passed 8 tests, and the full native gate passed 137 tests with one ignored manual probe in 346.99 seconds. The standalone Tauri Windows debug/no-bundle build passed with 1,931 frontend modules in 3.10 seconds and native compilation in 19.25 seconds. Focused UI tests and a mocked local-browser harness cover display/export-safe selections, blank and changed-only behavior, cancellation/error draft retention, stale and busy guards, and a clean output tab while retaining a dirty source. Native save-dialog, installed-app, and broader cross-viewer behavior remain unverified. This source work is newer than the unpublished 0.2.6 draft; see the [Forms guide](tools/forms.md) and [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Extended source-only **Fill forms** to a strict standard radio-group subset alongside text fields and checkboxes. The dialog shows source names, pages, and verbatim source option labels, while patches use opaque widget IDs; checkbox on-state names remain internal. It sends changed-only tagged selections, supports a parser-proven blank initial group, and cannot clear a radio choice. The full frontend suite passed 152 tests, the focused native radio gate passed 6 tests, and the full native gate passed 129 tests with one ignored manual probe. The standalone Tauri Windows debug/no-bundle build also passed. Focused UI tests and a mocked Vite browser harness cover independent groups, Arrow/Space selection, blank groups, changed-only selections, cancellation/error drafts, stale/busy guards, and a clean output tab without changing a dirty source. This source work is newer than the unpublished 0.2.6 draft; see the [Forms guide](tools/forms.md) and [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Extended source-only **Fill forms** to a strict flat checkbox subset alongside text fields. The dialog uses source field names and pages, sends explicit tagged changed-only text or boolean patches, and never exposes a checkbox’s source on-state name. Validated checkbox artwork and styles are retained; radio groups, signatures, and unsupported forms are refused as a whole. The source checks passed 151 frontend/release tests and 123 native tests with one ignored manual probe, the production frontend build, and a standalone Tauri Windows debug/no-bundle build. Focused UI checks and a mocked Vite browser harness cover keyboard checkbox input, false/blank/reverted patches, stale and busy guards, cancellation/error draft retention, and a clean output tab without changing a dirty source. Native coverage checks custom on-state names, checked/cleared pixels, original checkbox appearance/default preservation, crops/rotations, immutable print snapshot bitmaps, corpus source preservation, and receiver ownership. Native save-dialog, installed-app, and broader cross-viewer behavior remain unverified. This source work is newer than the unpublished 0.2.6 draft; see the [Forms guide](tools/forms.md) and [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Made the Fill forms dialog scroll within the viewport so a supported 256-field form keeps every field and its actions reachable.

- Added source-only **Fill forms** for a strict flat single-line text-field subset. It writes a new copy with changed printable-ASCII values and preserves the source tab; unsupported field trees, actions, signatures, encryption, appearances, layouts, and edited page plans are refused as a whole. The full source checks passed 150 frontend/release tests and 116 native tests with one ignored manual probe, the production frontend build, and the standalone Windows debug build. Focused UI checks and a mocked Vite browser harness cover changed-only patches, limits, stale and busy guards, cancellation/error draft retention, and a clean output tab without changing a dirty source; native regressions cover a ReportLab compatibility fixture, pypdf/Poppler inspection, rotations/crops, immutable print snapshot bitmaps, all corpus pages, and output/session guards. This source feature is newer than the unpublished 0.2.6 draft; see the [Forms guide](tools/forms.md) and [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Added source-only **selected-text highlights**. In Select, choose supported embedded text on one visible page, then choose **Highlight selected text**. The app maps the browser endpoints to complete native geometry characters, and native code saves multiline quads rather than browser rectangles. A text highlight can paint 256 quads; a document has 4,096 annotation geometry slots, and pages with more than 512 edit targets route to the Comments list without losing saved highlights. The full source gate passed 144 frontend/release tests and 108 native tests with one ignored manual probe, the production frontend build, and the standalone Windows debug build. Focused UI checks and a mocked browser harness cover multiline and keyboard selection, Unicode boundaries, stale/cross-page rejection, toolbar focus, and the target fallback; they do not prove native desktop saving, rendering, print, or installed-app behavior. This source feature is newer than the unpublished 0.2.6 draft; see the [Comments guide](tools/comments.md) and [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Removed an internal planning blueprint and detailed signing setup references from the current source tree. Master and historical version tags were rewritten to remove those details from reachable public branch/tag history, and historical v0.2.6 source references now point to the rewritten tag. Four identified Actions logs were privately archived and removed. Existing installers and signatures were not rebuilt or re-signed; their original provenance is retained in a private recovery archive. GitHub may still serve orphaned old-SHA content from caches or internal retention, so this is not a claim that every public copy was erased.

- Added source-only **area highlights** alongside sticky notes. Drag a fixed rectangle on the displayed page; it is not a text-selection highlight and cannot be resized after creation. Native PDF rendering owns the fixed yellow translucent appearance, while the UI supplies only transparent edit targets. Notes and highlights share the strict owned-annotation guards and limits, survive supported page operations, and stay list-editable when cropped out of view. The final gate passed 132 frontend/release tests, 99 native tests with one ignored manual probe, the production frontend build, and the standalone Windows debug build. Focused UI tests and a mocked browser drag prove client-rectangle mapping, cancellation, mode behavior, transparent targets, and optional Unicode bodies; they do not prove native desktop interaction, saved PDF output, printing, or installed-app behavior. See the [Comments guide](tools/comments.md) and [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Stabilized two existing high-work tests with individual 15-second budgets: the 500-page search cap and offline Cargo metadata/archive verification. They passed in isolation and together; the earlier full-run timeouts are evidence of scheduling-dependent deadlines, not a product performance measurement or a CPU-cause conclusion.

- Added source-only sticky-note comments. Comment mode creates one bounded note at a time; the Comments panel can select, edit, or delete visible and crop-hidden notes. Notes use a strict app-owned PDF schema, so foreign or protected annotations are refused rather than changed; Combine, Insert Pages, and Replace Pages also refuse annotated sources. The frontend/release suite passed 123 tests, the native suite passed 94 tests with one ignored manual probe, and the production frontend and standalone Windows debug builds passed. Focused UI tests and a mocked browser harness cover placement, transparent hit geometry, hidden-note editing, stale requests, busy/Escape behavior, and UTF-8 limits; they do not verify native desktop dialog, save, print, or installed-app behavior. See the [Comments guide](tools/comments.md) and [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Updated local test setup to fetch the locked Windows Cargo registry before frontend tests. This supplies the cache used by offline dependency-notice checks while keeping those checks fail-closed.

- Verified the signed, unpublished **v0.2.6** draft in [GitHub Actions run 35250878119](https://github.com/Joshua-Beel/Smacrobat/actions/runs/35250878119) before the privacy rewrite. The rewritten [`v0.2.6` tag](https://github.com/Joshua-Beel/Smacrobat/commit/8cf90166623857dddd3854ce769549da55f6f5c4) records the cleaned source history; the existing draft assets were not rebuilt or re-signed. The three draft assets, updater manifest and negative controls, three publisher signatures (installer, application, and PDFium DLL), 26 extracted resources, five MPL archives, and bundled notices were checked; the installer SHA-256 is `ec47981b533fdf4eb00d9fe62e52edd7bc1ef160cda9c18d962f0e1f39dfe255`. This does not establish installation, upgrade, relaunch, or installed-app behavior. The historical [v0.2.5 workflow](https://github.com/Joshua-Beel/Smacrobat/actions/runs/35250005735) stopped during npm tests before native tests, signing, installer creation, asset upload, or release creation. See [release notes](release-notes.md) and [releases and signing](releases.md).

- Independent adversarial review ran 29 modal/native-save focused checks and 17 updater, release-manifest, preferences, and recent-files focused checks without a new finding; installer and relaunch paths received a separate code review. These are focused evidence, not a final review.

- Added **Replace pages** in Organize Pages. It creates a new copy by substituting one contiguous target range with every current edited donor page; target Info/XMP metadata is retained even when the full target range is replaced, and both source tabs keep their state. The replacement source gate passed 109 frontend/release tests, 85 native tests with one separately ignored manual probe, the production frontend build, and a standalone Windows debug build. A mocked-IPC browser harness checked the range preview, submitted revisions, and derived output count, not the native save dialog. A prior interrupted native run had no test-result footer; its cause remains unconfirmed and the captured rerun passed. This source feature is included in the unpublished 0.2.6 draft; see [review findings](review-findings.md) and the [Organize Pages guide](tools/organize-pages.md).

- Added **Insert pages** in Organize Pages. It creates a new copy by placing all current edited donor pages at a chosen boundary in a distinct target PDF; target Info/XMP metadata is retained and both source tabs keep their state. The source gate passed 102 frontend/release tests, 79 native tests, the production frontend build, and a standalone Windows debug build. Native coverage checks ordered fixture pairs, output pages, rotated/cropped content, source history, and publication guards; a mocked-IPC browser harness checked the boundary preview and submitted revisions, not the Windows save dialog. See [review findings](review-findings.md) and the [Organize Pages guide](tools/organize-pages.md).

- Moved the canonical repository and updater endpoints to [Smacrobat](https://github.com/Joshua-Beel/Smacrobat). The former update URL currently redirects to this repository; future manifests and update checks now use the canonical address directly.

- Added exact source archives for five locked MPL-2.0 crates to the source build's bundled resources, with manifest hashes, official archive locations, cache/source-tree verification, and installer freshness checks. A fresh unsigned debug installer extraction matched all five archive hashes plus both notice files; it does not verify installation, updates, signatures, or a release installer. Native render cleanup now lets Close and EndPrint move ahead of a bounded run of queued viewer renders while preserving all other command ordering. The source gate passed 95 frontend tests, 73 native tests, the production frontend build, and a standalone unsigned-debug NSIS build; see [review findings](review-findings.md) and the [license inventory](license-inventory.md).

- Hardened native worker-reply cleanup. An abandoned raw Open, BeginOpen, Unlock, or Combine result now releases only its newly registered session, and an abandoned password prompt clears its pending token; accepted results remain usable and an unclaimed combined file stays saved. This covers the worker-to-service handoff, not later WebView or JavaScript receipt. The source checks passed 69 native tests, the unchanged 92 frontend/release tests, the production frontend build, and the standalone Windows debug build; see [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Hardened native print and open-reply cleanup. Dropping an unread completed print reply no longer occupies one of four snapshot slots, and an ordinary open whose reply closes before delivery no longer leaves an unreachable document; the print job keeps its snapshot through spool-thread handoff, completion, and panic. The source gate passed 92 frontend/release tests, 63 native tests, the production frontend build, and the standalone Windows debug build. Native printer output and driver cancellation still need verification; see [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Combine Files now joins two distinct open PDFs in the selected order into a new copy, retaining the first PDF’s Info/XMP metadata and leaving both source tabs unchanged. It accepts current edits, rejects unsupported structure, and limits output to 4,096 pages and 256 MiB; those output limits are not an application memory cap. The source gate passed 92 frontend/release tests, 60 native tests, the production frontend build, and the standalone Windows debug build; a mocked-IPC browser harness checked order, page count, metadata notice, and submitted revisions, not the native save dialog. See [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Organize Pages now starts from the page you are viewing and can crop one selected page with point insets and a resulting-size preview. Cropping hides content rather than redacting it; invalid or oversized insets, stale documents, failures, and duplicate submissions leave the session intact. The source gate passed 87 frontend/release tests, 52 native tests, the production frontend build, and the standalone Windows debug build; a mocked-IPC browser harness checked the crop preview and normalized request, while native tests checked rendered-pixel and source preservation. See the [Organize Pages guide](tools/organize-pages.md) and [review findings](review-findings.md).

- Search now highlights literal matches directly on visible pages with supported glyph geometry while preserving Select copy behavior; Pan keeps highlights but disables selection. Query changes, stopped work, document tabs, and revisions clear or constrain overlay state, and a matching page whose geometry cannot locate text says so instead of drawing a guessed box. The final source gate passed 80 frontend/release tests and the production build; a mocked-IPC browser harness verified 0°/90° boxes and selected `A B\né`, not Windows clipboard or real native IPC. See [review findings](review-findings.md) and the [development roadmap](development-roadmap.md).

- Fixed Page text and Find so cropped or rotated pages retain visible headings and exclude text outside the crop. The regression failed before the fix and passed across all 16 original/edited rotation combinations afterward; all 45 native tests pass.

- Added fixed-size PDF splitting in Organize Pages. It writes the current edited page order to a new Windows-selected folder only after every output validates; source bytes, unsaved state, and undo/redo history remain unchanged. The combined source gate passed 72 frontend/release tests, 44 native tests, the production frontend build, and the standalone Windows debug build; native folder-dialog interaction remains unverified. See the [Organize Pages guide](tools/organize-pages.md) and [review findings](review-findings.md) for output, preservation, and size limits.

- Added on-page text selection for supported embedded text: Select enables it, Pan disables it, and unsupported, capped, malformed, or no-positioned-geometry pages offer Page text. The combined gate passed 66 frontend/release tests, 38 native tests, the production frontend build, and the standalone Windows debug build; a mocked-IPC browser harness verified aligned ranges and selected `A B\né`, but not the Windows clipboard or real native IPC. The feature is not included in published 0.2.0 or the 0.2.4 draft; see [review findings](review-findings.md) and the [development roadmap](development-roadmap.md).

- Added Document properties, including metadata, current page dimensions and reported permissions. Unknown signature counts stay unknown instead of displaying a library error as 65,535 signatures. Fixed a close-tab race that could leave a blank workspace, and blocked edits/export on malformed PDFs that reference the same page object twice. Added a reproducible 367-package dependency-notice collection, an app menu entry and an installer freshness check. All 59 frontend/release tests, 32 native tests, the production build and the standalone Windows debug build pass. Both copied notice resources match their source SHA-256 hashes. MPL source availability, the project's own license (since set to MIT; see `LICENSE`) and new installer verification remained open at that point; see the [inventory](license-inventory.md).

- Added native Windows printing in the source build: printer/range selection, bounded page-image rendering from a stable edited-document snapshot, cancellation and cleanup on failures. Printing never clears unsaved edits and rejects encrypted files. Review fixed early/late cancellation races and a page-limit mismatch. Undo/redo snapshots now have a combined 32 MiB budget per document. All 49 frontend/release tests, 27 native tests and the production build pass. The [dependency inventory](license-inventory.md) records license evidence and remaining packaging work. Native printer output, driver behavior and installer upgrade remain unverified; see [printing notes](printing-plan.md). These changes are newer than the 0.2.4 draft.

- Tab switching now restores each document's last viewed page and clamps it after page edits shorten the document. Expanded native password tests to six RC4-128, AES-128 and AES-256 fixtures with protected and empty passwords, checking every rendered page, retries, cancellation, edit/export rejection and original-byte preservation. All 45 frontend/release tests, 14 native tests and the production build pass. Native interaction remains unverified. Printing remains disabled; [implementation notes](printing-plan.md) describe the native path needed before enabling it.

- Parallel password and review work added opening-password prompts with retry/cancel, transient passwords, and cleanup after canceled opens. Encrypted files remain read-only, including empty-password files that lopdf automatically decrypts. Fixed silently truncated malformed PDFs, blocked edits/export when PDF engines disagree on the source page count, limited opening to the IPC-supported 65,536 pages, and disabled background shortcuts inside confirmation dialogs. Added interrupted-update/retry tests. All 43 frontend/release tests, 14 native tests and the production build pass. Native password interaction and the installer upgrade remain unverified. See [review findings](review-findings.md) for remaining risks; these source changes are newer than the 0.2.4 draft.

- Recent files and stars now persist locally, capped at 50 entries. Clicking a saved entry reopens it or activates its existing tab; missing files show an error. Clear file history removes saved locations without deleting PDFs. Tests cover invalid storage, duplicate entries, app remounts, reopening and missing files. All 33 frontend/release tests, 11 native tests and the production build pass. Native restart verification remains pending; this feature is newer than the 0.2.4 draft.

- Added next/previous matching-page controls and highlighted literal matches in search excerpts. Navigation wraps through the available results and resets when the query or case option changes. New tests cover navigation, delayed results after Stop, and the 500-page result limit; all 29 frontend/release tests and the production build pass. Highlighting on rendered pages and native desktop verification remain pending. This change is newer than the 0.2.4 draft.

- Added Move to page in Organize Pages: choose one page, enter its final position, and move it using the existing undoable operation. Invalid positions are rejected and failed moves preserve selection. Fixed Shift+click selecting nonexistent pages after deletion. All 26 frontend/release tests and the production build pass; native desktop interaction with the new controls remains unverified. These changes are newer than the 0.2.4 draft.

- Fixed an export overflow found with adversarial PDF rotation values. The regression reproduced a debug-build panic; export now normalizes the original rotation before adding the requested turn. Extreme positive and negative values round-trip correctly, and all 11 native tests pass across the existing fixtures. This source fix is newer than the 0.2.4 draft; the full review and installed-app checks remain open.

- Added a page-text dialog for selecting embedded text and copying with Ctrl+C. It distinguishes empty pages from extraction errors and displays PDF text without interpreting it as HTML. All 23 frontend/release tests and the production build pass. Native clipboard interaction is still unverified; this feature is newer than the 0.2.4 draft and does not provide selection directly over rendered pages.

- Adversarial bookmark tests exposed repeated PDFium initialization and interference between parallel native workers: tests found inconsistent bookmark counts, preview mismatches, and startup failures. All service handles now share one process-wide worker, serializing initialization and PDF operations. Added cases for cyclic outlines, 1,005 bookmarks, empty outlines, closed documents, and internal GoTo actions; bookmark results remain capped at 1,000. This is an incremental hardening pass, not completion of the requested full review.

- Added a native bookmark pane with internal page navigation, nested entries, an explicit 1,000-entry cap, and disabled unsupported destinations. All 21 frontend/release tests, 9 native tests, and the production build pass, including nested bookmark extraction, external-action rejection, stale requests, and UI navigation. Native desktop interaction remains unverified. This change is newer than the 0.2.4 draft.

- The source build now saves reading preferences locally: theme, zoom, fit mode, pan mode, and panel visibility. Invalid saved values fall back to defaults, and unavailable storage shows a session-only notice. All 19 frontend/release tests and the production build pass, including an app remount test and storage failure cases. These changes are newer than the 0.2.4 draft; native restart verification is still pending.

- The 0.2.4 release workflow passed all tests, signed the installer, verified its packaged application, and created a draft release. Downloaded assets were independently checked locally: both publisher signatures are valid and timestamped; installer SHA-256 is `cf97903897c10f0f02c31bc61f9ac9d929a9c5abdc116dc200cce01a4cd68c7a`, matching GitHub. Desktop automation still returns unreliable window captures, so installation/search UI checks and the full upgrade test remain unverified. The release stays a draft.

- The 0.2.3 signature check found Tauri's restored unsigned build file. CI now verifies the installer, extracts its packaged application using 7-Zip, and verifies that application's timestamped publisher signature. Prepared 0.2.4 for this check. Publishing and the upgrade test are still pending.

- Final completion requires a deep code review and adversarial tests: malformed PDFs, preservation of original files, undo/redo branches, stale search results, resource limits, crash behavior, and interrupted or invalid updates. Reproducible failures must be fixed with regression tests; unverified cases and remaining risks must be reported.

- Azure signing succeeded for 0.2.2, including the installer and updater signature, but CI stopped when the Windows PowerShell child process could not load its signature-verification module. Changed CI to call the build script directly in PowerShell 7 and prepared 0.2.3. Full signature verification, publishing, and the upgrade test remain pending.

- The 0.2.1 CI build passed tests and compilation but failed when invoking the signing tool. Fixed the signing configuration to pass the spaced application description as one argument, using Tauri's object notation. Prepared 0.2.2, including document search, for another signed build; installer and upgrade verification remain pending. The failed 0.2.1 tag is preserved.

- Added embedded-text search to the source build, with matching-page navigation, case matching, cancellation, selectable excerpts, and protection against stale results. All 15 frontend/release and 8 native tests pass; native extraction checks every page of all three valid fixtures. Production frontend and native debug builds pass. Desktop interaction verification is pending because window automation failed to acquire reliable input. This feature is not included in the already-tagged 0.2.1 signing build.

- Prepared 0.2.1 to exercise Azure publisher signing and the upgrade from 0.2.0. Release verification is in progress; this is not yet a verified published installer.

- Configured the release secrets and an Azure client credential in GitHub Actions. The next release build will test signing end to end.

- Rewrote this README around installing and using the app, with clearer setup instructions and less development-log clutter.

- Connected future release builds to Azure publisher signing. The build checks for valid, timestamped publisher signatures before preparing an update. The published 0.2.0 installer hasn't changed.

- Released the 0.2.0 installer and GitHub updater. Verified installation, PDF rendering, the live update check, and the downloaded installer's hash. Fixed update links to match GitHub's asset filenames. A full upgrade to a newer version still needs an end-to-end test. Details are in the [release guide](releases.md).

- Added Organize Pages, undo/redo, Save a Copy, and prompts for unsaved edits. Tests compare rotated previews with reopened saved files and check that originals remain unchanged. The latest recorded checks passed all 11 frontend/release tests and 7 Rust tests; desktop rotation and copy export were also checked. See the [tool guide](tools/organize-pages.md).

- Built the initial viewer, native PDF worker, page cache, and test fixtures. Added a standalone development build and shortcut, and fixed a Windows file-lock issue in the development watcher. Earlier checks are recorded in [Viewer foundation](phase-0-status.md).
